Results · Definitions

← suite simplipy

sp-acj4-147-cond contingent

category simplipy-conditional

Summary

Given
\(x \in \mathbb{C},\quad x \in i\mathbb{R},\quad \cosh\left(\operatorname{arsinh}\left(x\right)\right) \neq 0,\quad x \neq 0,\quad \frac{1}{x} \neq i,\quad \frac{1}{x} \neq -i\)
Claim
\(\displaystyle \left|\tanh\left(\operatorname{arsinh}\left(x\right)\right)\right| \overset{?}{=} \cos\left(\arctan\left(\frac{1}{x}\right)\right)\)
Expected
contingent true at \(x = 2 \, i\); false at \(x = \frac{1}{2} \, i\)

equal at some admissible points, different at others

witness (true): \(x = 2 \, i\)

witness (false): \(x = \frac{1}{2} \, i\)

SimpliPy acj-4 (MIT) rules_real.json[147]: abs tanh asinh _0 -> cos atan inv _0 [weakest assumption found: imaginary]; relabelled contingent: false at x = (* 1/2 i) (atan is specified on its branch cuts since harness PR #19)

Lean proof: included

Source (conditional.sexp)

(case sp-acj4-147-cond (category simplipy-conditional)
  (note "SimpliPy acj-4 (MIT) rules_real.json[147]: abs tanh asinh _0  ->  cos atan inv _0  [weakest assumption found: imaginary]; relabelled contingent: false at x = (* 1/2 i) (atan is specified on its branch cuts since harness PR #19)")
  (var x complex)
  (assume (imaginary x))
  (assume (nonzero (cosh (asinh x))))
  (assume (nonzero x))
  (assume (!= (/ x) i))
  (assume (!= (/ x) (- i)))
  (ask (= (abs (tanh (asinh x))) (cos (atan (/ x)))))
  (expect contingent)
  (witness true (x (* 2 i)) (lean-proof "exact AE.w3_c147_w1"))
  (witness false (x (* 1/2 i)) (lean-proof "exact AE.w3_c147_w2")))

Lean proofs (2; checked by lean-check, each under the statement it proves)

witness true at x = 2i
⊢imaginary(2 · i)
∧nonzero(cosh(asinh(2 · i)))
∧nonzero(2 · i)
∧1 / (2 · i) ≠ i
∧1 / (2 · i) ≠ −i
∧|tanh(asinh(2 · i))| = cos(atan(1 / (2 · i)))
definedness side conditions (4, and 6 trivially true; t↓: t is defined)
⊢imaginary(2 · i)↓
⊢asinh(2 · i)↓ ×2 trivially true
⊢cosh(asinh(2 · i))↓ trivially true
⊢nonzero(cosh(asinh(2 · i)))↓ trivially true
⊢nonzero(2 · i)↓ trivially true
⊢2 · i ≠ 0 ×3
⊢tanh(asinh(2 · i))↓
⊢|tanh(asinh(2 · i))|↓ trivially true
⊢atan(1 / (2 · i))↓
⊢cos(atan(1 / (2 · i)))↓ trivially true
Lean statement
theorem case_sp_acj4_147_cond_witness1 : (((AE.imaginary.D (2 * AE.i)) ∧ (AE.imaginary (2 * AE.i))) ∧ (((AE.asinh.D (2 * AE.i) ∧ AE.cosh.D (AE.asinh (2 * AE.i))) ∧ AE.nonzero.D (AE.cosh (AE.asinh (2 * AE.i)))) ∧ (AE.nonzero (AE.cosh (AE.asinh (2 * AE.i))))) ∧ ((AE.nonzero.D (2 * AE.i)) ∧ (AE.nonzero (2 * AE.i))) ∧ ((2 * AE.i ≠ 0) ∧ (AE.inv (2 * AE.i) ≠ AE.i)) ∧ ((2 * AE.i ≠ 0) ∧ (AE.inv (2 * AE.i) ≠ -AE.i)) ∧ ((((AE.asinh.D (2 * AE.i) ∧ AE.tanh.D (AE.asinh (2 * AE.i))) ∧ AE.abs.D (AE.tanh (AE.asinh (2 * AE.i)))) ∧ (2 * AE.i ≠ 0 ∧ AE.atan.D (AE.inv (2 * AE.i))) ∧ AE.cos.D (AE.atan (AE.inv (2 * AE.i)))) ∧ (AE.abs (AE.tanh (AE.asinh (2 * AE.i))) = AE.cos (AE.atan (AE.inv (2 * AE.i)))))) :=
exact AE.w3_c147_w1
witness false at x = (1/2)i
⊢imaginary(1/2 · i)
∧nonzero(cosh(asinh(1/2 · i)))
∧nonzero(1/2 · i)
∧1 / (1/2 · i) ≠ i
∧1 / (1/2 · i) ≠ −i
∧|tanh(asinh(1/2 · i))| ≠ cos(atan(1 / (1/2 · i)))
definedness side conditions (4, and 6 trivially true; t↓: t is defined)
⊢imaginary(1/2 · i)↓
⊢asinh(1/2 · i)↓ ×2 trivially true
⊢cosh(asinh(1/2 · i))↓ trivially true
⊢nonzero(cosh(asinh(1/2 · i)))↓ trivially true
⊢nonzero(1/2 · i)↓ trivially true
⊢1/2 · i ≠ 0 ×3
⊢tanh(asinh(1/2 · i))↓
⊢|tanh(asinh(1/2 · i))|↓ trivially true
⊢atan(1 / (1/2 · i))↓
⊢cos(atan(1 / (1/2 · i)))↓ trivially true
Lean statement
theorem case_sp_acj4_147_cond_witness2 : (((AE.imaginary.D (1 / 2 * AE.i)) ∧ (AE.imaginary (1 / 2 * AE.i))) ∧ (((AE.asinh.D (1 / 2 * AE.i) ∧ AE.cosh.D (AE.asinh (1 / 2 * AE.i))) ∧ AE.nonzero.D (AE.cosh (AE.asinh (1 / 2 * AE.i)))) ∧ (AE.nonzero (AE.cosh (AE.asinh (1 / 2 * AE.i))))) ∧ ((AE.nonzero.D (1 / 2 * AE.i)) ∧ (AE.nonzero (1 / 2 * AE.i))) ∧ ((1 / 2 * AE.i ≠ 0) ∧ (AE.inv (1 / 2 * AE.i) ≠ AE.i)) ∧ ((1 / 2 * AE.i ≠ 0) ∧ (AE.inv (1 / 2 * AE.i) ≠ -AE.i)) ∧ ((((AE.asinh.D (1 / 2 * AE.i) ∧ AE.tanh.D (AE.asinh (1 / 2 * AE.i))) ∧ AE.abs.D (AE.tanh (AE.asinh (1 / 2 * AE.i)))) ∧ (1 / 2 * AE.i ≠ 0 ∧ AE.atan.D (AE.inv (1 / 2 * AE.i))) ∧ AE.cos.D (AE.atan (AE.inv (1 / 2 * AE.i)))) ∧ (AE.abs (AE.tanh (AE.asinh (1 / 2 * AE.i))) ≠ AE.cos (AE.atan (AE.inv (1 / 2 * AE.i)))))) :=

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
# the claim as a relation, sympy-extras' statement form (not Q.zero: see the adapter)
Answer = sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(Residual, 0), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

Times: wall-clock seconds per query as the runner measured them (each query in its own forked process; setup and warm-up not timed). Machines: pi5 (indicative): it has some other load: times are a fair guide, large differences between its runs mean something, small ones may be noise (Raspberry Pi 5, 4 cores. Benchmark runs go one at a time, in a low-priority systemd unit (CPUWeight=20, Nice=10); since 2026-10-17, each eval is pinned to core 3 (earlier runs were not pinned). The Pi also serves websites, and other work may share core 3 unless it is reserved for the runs (pi5-deploy INSTALL.md, step 15). Clock frequency and temperature are not controlled).

// weakened: (imaginary x) (imaginary (no Giac type); dropped)
// weakened: (nonzero (cosh (asinh x))) (not a predicate of one variable; dropped)
// weakened: (!= (/ x) i) (not a predicate of one variable; dropped)
// weakened: (!= (/ x) (- i)) (not a predicate of one variable; dropped)

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
from sympy.assumptions.satask import satask
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.trigsimp(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, Q.real(W), strict=True)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
Result = satsimp.trigsimp(Residual, Assum, strict=True)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceAbs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))→Abs(x)/Abs(sqrt(x**2 + 1)) - 1/sqrt(1 + x**(-2))
satsimp.trigsimpsatsimp.trigsimp(Residual, Assum, strict=True)→Abs(x)/Abs(sqrt(x**2 + 1)) - 1/sqrt(1 + x**(-2))
answernull

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(satsimp.refine(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, Q.positive(W), strict=True))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
Result = simplify(satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum, strict=True))
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

satsimp.refine+simplifysimplify(satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum, strict=True))→Abs(x/sqrt(x**2 + 1)) - 1/sqrt(1 + x**(-2))

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satassume.sympy_api
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satassume')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satassume.sympy_api.ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W), strict=True)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
Answer = satassume.sympy_api.ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum, strict=True)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

(
  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)
(* evaluated in a forked copy of the setup kernel: TimeConstrained would not fire here (no timer thread), so the parent enforces a hard timeout of 9 s by killing this process *)

  Assum = (Element[x, Complexes]) && ((Re[x] == 0 && x != 0)) && ((Cosh[ArcSinh[x]] != 0)) && ((x != 0)) && (((1/x) != I)) && (((1/x) != (-I)));
  Residual = (Abs[Tanh[ArcSinh[x]]]) - (Cos[ArcTan[1/x]]);
  Result = FullSimplify[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, imaginary=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x)))
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False)
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
Result = simplify(Residual)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
Result = refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sqrt(8) + sin(-W) + exp(log(W)) - Abs(-W)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, imaginary=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x)))
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False)
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
# automatic evaluation only: no simplify, refine or other function
Result = Residual
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
from sympy.assumptions.satask import satask
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.refine(Abs(W - 1) + sqrt(W**2), W > 2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'

W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))

print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, imaginary=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x)))
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False)
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceAbs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))→Abs(x)/Abs(sqrt(x**2 + 1)) - 1/sqrt(1 + x**(-2))
sympy_extras.assumptions.simplifysympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)→Abs(x)/Abs(sqrt(x**2 + 1)) - 1/sqrt(1 + x**(-2))

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
# the claim as a relation, sympy-extras' statement form (not Q.zero: see the adapter)
Answer = sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(Residual, 0), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

import sympy

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(satsimp.refine(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, Q.positive(W)))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, imaginary=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x)))
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
Result = simplify(satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum))
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceAbs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))→Abs(x)/Abs(sqrt(x**2 + 1)) - 1/sqrt(1 + x**(-2))
satsimp.refine+simplifysimplify(satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum))→-sqrt(-x**2)/sqrt(-x**2 - 1) + Abs(x/sqrt(x**2 + 1))
answernull

import sympy

import satassume.sympy_api
import importlib.metadata

# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:

Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))

# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];

  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)

  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sqrt(8) + sin(-W) + exp(log(W)) - Abs(-W)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, imaginary=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x)))
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False)
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
# automatic evaluation only: no simplify, refine or other function
Result = Residual
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satrefine
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satrefine')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satrefine.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
Result = satrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceAbs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))→Abs(x)/Abs(sqrt(x**2 + 1)) - 1/sqrt(1 + x**(-2))
satrefine.refinesatrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)→Abs(x)/sqrt(Abs(x**2 + 1)) - 1/sqrt(1 + x**(-2))

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, imaginary=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x)))
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False)
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
Result = simplify(Residual)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))

# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

import sympy

W = Symbol('W')

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, imaginary=True, zero=False)

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satrefine
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satrefine')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satrefine.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
Result = satrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

satrefine.refinesatrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)→Abs(x)/sqrt(Abs(x**2 + 1)) - 1/sqrt(1 + x**(-2))

import sympy

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, imaginary=True, zero=False)

# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False)

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
(
  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)

  Assum = (Element[x, Complexes]) && ((Re[x] == 0 && x != 0)) && ((Cosh[ArcSinh[x]] != 0)) && ((x != 0)) && (((1/x) != I)) && (((1/x) != (-I)));
  Residual = (Abs[Tanh[ArcSinh[x]]]) - (Cos[ArcTan[1/x]]);
  Result = FullSimplify[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, imaginary=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x)))
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False)
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
Result = simplify(Residual)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
Result = refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
(
  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)
